<h3><a href="https://www.threads.com/@saars.foto/post/DezJ5OFEcU7">Haunted Thursday 🎃 Show me your Halloween. Pumpkin patches, porch carvings, decorations, foggy corners, odd shadows - festive or full-on creepy, all of it counts. This big guy is on pumpkin duty all October. ONE photo. Best of the week lands on Instagram on the weekend.</a></h3>
